<script type="text/javascript">
        //取得mem_uid的cookie
        function getCookie(){
        cookieArray = document.cookie.split(";")
        for(var i = 0;i < cookieArray.length;i++){
        	var temp = cookieArray[i].split("=");
        	if(temp[0].match('mem_uid')) 
        		return unescape(temp[1]);
        }
    }


rtuserid = getCookie();//uesrID
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
	function checkPasswd(passwd)
	{
		if( passwd.length < 6 || passwd.length > 12 ) {
			alert("密碼需 8 ~ 12 碼，建議使用英數混和，請勿使用!、~等特殊字元");
			return false;
		}
		
		var aa=0; // 小寫英文字
		var bb=0; // 大寫英文字
		var cc=0; // 數字
		var dd=0; // 其他符號
		for (var i = 0 ; i < passwd.length ; i++ ) {
			if(( passwd.charAt(i)>= 'a' && passwd.charAt(i) <= 'z' )) {
				aa++;
			} else if (( passwd.charAt(i)>= '0' && passwd.charAt(i) <= '9' )){
				bb++; 
			} else if (passwd.charAt(i)>= 'A' && passwd.charAt(i) <= 'Z') {
				cc++;
			} else {
				dd++;
			}
		}
		// var mem_account = '';
		//if (passwd.substring(0, mem_account.length) == mem_account) {
		//	alert('您的密碼內包括帳號訊息!');
		//	return false;
		//}
		//else if (passwd.length > mem_account.length && passwd.substring(passwd.length-mem_account.length) == mem_account) {
		//	alert('您的密碼內包括帳號訊息!');
		//	return false;
		//}
		
		// if(parseInt(aa)+parseInt(bb)>=7 && aa!=0 && bb!=0 || parseInt(bb)+parseInt(cc)>=7 && bb!=0 && cc!=0 || parseInt(aa)+parseInt(bb)+parseInt(cc)>=7 && aa!=0 && bb!=0 && cc!=0){ 
		if (aa + cc >= 1 && bb >= 1) {
			/*	var dictionaryArray = dictionaryString.split('|');
			
			if (bb < 6) {
				for (i = 0; i < dictionaryArray.length; i ++) {
					if (passwd.substring(0, dictionaryArray[i].length) == dictionaryArray[i]) {
						alert('您的密碼內包括字典常用字!');
						return false;
					}
					else if (passwd.length > dictionaryArray[i].length && passwd.substring(passwd.length-dictionaryArray[i].length) == dictionaryArray[i]) {
						alert('您的密碼內包括字典常用字!');
						return false;
					}
				}
			}
			*/
			// alert("OK");
			return true;
		} else { 
			alert("您的密碼只能是英文字母加數字組合，不能含空白鍵、*、@、#、%或其他符號 !");
			return false;
		}
	}
</script>

<script language="javascript">
//<![CDATA[
    /*熱門關鍵字*/
    function hot_keyword(name)
    {
        $("#prod_keyword").val(name);
        document.topForm.submit();
    }
    /*熱門關鍵字END*/

    /*url*/
    $(document).ready(function(){
      $("#btn_submit").click(function(){
            $("[name='topForm']").attr("action","index.php?action=prod_search&prod_keyword="+$("[name='prod_keyword']").val());
        });
    });
    /*url end*/

    /*抓購物車商品圖片*/
    function getProdImg(uid)
    {
        var _return;
        $.ajax({
            url : "/direct/api/api.shopping_cart.php",
            type : "POST",
            data : {
                myAct : "getProdImg",
                uid : uid
            },
            async:false,
            success : function(data)
            {
                _return = data;
            }
        });
        return _return;
    }
    /*抓購物車商品圖片 END*/
    /*移除購物車商品*/
    function it45_removeScar( uid ){
        $.ajax({
            url : "/direct/api/api.shopping_cart.php",
            type : "POST",
            data : {
                myAct : "removeScarByUid",
                uid : uid
            },
            success : function(data)
            {
                top_refresh();
            }
        });
    }
    /*移除購物車商品END*/

    function _topFormCheck(_this) {
        if (_this.prod_keyword.value == "") {
            alert('請輸入關鍵字！');//請輸入關鍵字！
            return false;
        }
    }
    String.prototype.trim = function() {
        return this.replace(/(^\s*)|(\s*$)/g, "");
    }
    function formatNumber(str) {
        str = str.trim();

        if (str.length <= 3) {
            return str;
        } else {
            return formatNumber(str.substr(0, str.length - 3)) + ','
                    + str.substr(str.length - 3);
        }
    }
    var top_scar_no = 0;

    function getCookie(name) {
        var nameEQ = name + "=";
        var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
        for ( var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
            var c = ca[i];
            while (c.charAt(0) == ' ')
                c = c.substring(1, c.length);
            if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) == 0)
                return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
        }
        return null;
    }

    function top_refresh() {
        var _scar_no = "";
        if (_scar_no == '') {
            _scar_no = getCookie('scar_no_' + $(location).attr('href').split('/')[3]);

        }
        $.ajax({
                    url : "/direct/api/api.shopping_cart.php",
                    type : "POST",
                    dataType : "json",
                    data : {
                        myAct : "getCartNumber",
                        scar_no : _scar_no,
                        nti_JTdFeiVCMyVBQiVBRCU3QyVCQyVBRCVBRCVCMCVCQyVCNHh6JUE5JUFFJUJEJUFBJTdEJUIxJUI4JUJCJTdEJUI3JUFFeQ : "MTU3MTgwMTk3OHlpOFFaTm1QRnFmQU5pNVFVVmdQNlI2c0diZVdqNUN6"
                    },
                    success : function(Jdata) {
                        if (Jdata.error == 'C00') {
                            $('#top_scar_no').html(Jdata.count);
                            $('#top_scar_no2').html(Jdata.count);
                            $('#sideCart3').empty();
                            var html = "";
                            var top_sum = 0;
                            $.each(
                                            Jdata.content,
                                            function(key, value) {
                                                //var subsum = parseInt(value['prod_selling_price'])*parseInt(value['scar_count']);
                                                var top_str = "";
                                                if (value['scar_type'] == 1)
                                                    top_str = "(加贈)";
                                                if (value['scar_type'] == 2)
                                                    top_str = "(加購)";
                                                var top_size = " ("
                                                        + value['prod_color_name']
                                                        + value['prod_size']
                                                        + ") ";
                                                var val = 'https://www.rt-mart.com.tw/website/uploads_product/' + value['prod_file'];
                                                //getProdImg(value['prod_uid']);
                                                html += '<ul class="pro">';
                                                html += '<li class="pro_img">';
                                                html += '<a href="/direct/index.php?action=product_detail&prod_no='
                                                        + value['prod_no']
                                                        + '">';
                                                html += '<img src="' + val + '" title="' + value['prod_name'] + '" alt="' + value['prod_name'] + '" width="45" height="45" />';
                                                html += '</a>';
                                                html += '</li>';
                                                html += '<li class="pro_name">';
                                                html += '<h5>';
                                                html += '<a href="/direct/index.php?action=product_detail&prod_no='
                                                        + value['prod_no']
                                                        + '">' + value['prod_name'] + '</a>';
                                                html += '</h5>';
                                                html += '<div class="price">$' + value['scar_price'] + '<span>X' + value['scar_count'] + '</span></div>';
                                                html += '</li>';
                                                html += '<li class="pro_del"><a href="javascript: it45_removeScar(\'' + value['uid'] + '\');">刪除</a></li>';
                                                html += '</ul>';

                                                top_sum += value['scar_price'] * value['scar_count'];
                                            });
                            if(top_sum > 0){
                                html += '<div class="checkout">';
                                html += '<div class="price">總計<!--總計-->：<span>$' + top_sum + '</span></div>';
                                html += '<div class="cart"><a href="/direct/index.php?action=shopping_cart&web_cart_myAct=SHOPPING_CART">結帳</a></div>';
                                html += '</div>';
                            }
                            $('#sideCart3').append(html);
                        }
                    }
                })
        $(".formatNumber").each(function(i) {
            var _num = $(this).html();
            $(this).html(formatNumber(_num));
        })
    }
    jQuery(document).ready(function($) {
        top_refresh();
    })


    // 捲軸滑動固定搜尋列
    $(function(){
    $left_nav = $(".left_nav"),
    $search = $(".search"),
    $body = $(document),
    $win = $(window);

        window.goTopMove = function () {
            var scrollH = $body.scrollTop();

                if(scrollH > 50) {

                    $left_nav.stop().css({"display":"none"});
                    $search.stop().css({"position":"fixed","top":"0px","right":"unset","z-index":"9999","margin-left":"56px"});
                    $(".hamburger").stop().css({"display":"unset"});
                    $("#rtLogo a").stop().css({"display":"inline-block"});

                }else{
                
                    $left_nav.stop().css({"display":"block"});
                    $search.stop().css({"position":"absolute","top":"40px","right":"0px","z-index":"unset","margin-left":"unset"});
                    $(".hamburger").stop().css({"display":"none"});
                    $("#rtLogo a").stop().css({"display":"none"});
                }
        };  
        $win.on({
            scroll: function() {goTopMove();},
            resize: function() {goTopMove();}
        });

        $(".hamburger").on("click",function(){
            $(".rtmartHEADER02").hasClass("segmentFixed")? $(".rtmartHEADER02").removeClass("segmentFixed"):$(".rtmartHEADER02").addClass("segmentFixed");
            $(".hamburger").hasClass("hamburgerFixed")? $(".hamburger").removeClass("hamburgerFixed"):$(".hamburger").addClass("hamburgerFixed");
        });

    });

<script type="text/javascript">
	$(function(){
		// 先取得 div#news_marquee ul
		// 接著把 ul 中的 li 項目再重覆加入 ul 中(等於有兩組內容)
		// 再來取得 div#news_marquee 的高來決定每次跑馬燈移動的距離
		// 設定跑馬燈移動的速度及輪播的速度
		var $marqueeUl = $('div#news_marquee ul'),
			$marqueeli = $marqueeUl.append($marqueeUl.html()).children(),
			_height = $('div#news_marquee').height() * -1,
			scrollSpeed = 600,
			timer,
			speed = 3000 + scrollSpeed,
			direction = 0,	// 0 表示往上, 1 表示往下
			_lock = false;

		// 先把 $marqueeli 移動到第二組
		$marqueeUl.css('top', $marqueeli.length / 2 * _height);
		
		// 幫左邊 $marqueeli 加上 hover 事件
		// 當滑鼠移入時停止計時器；反之則啟動
		$marqueeli.hover(function(){
			clearTimeout(timer);
		}, function(){
			timer = setTimeout(showad, speed);
		});
		
		// 判斷要往上還是往下
		$('div#news_marquee .marquee_btn').click(function(){
			if(_lock) return;
			clearTimeout(timer);
			direction = $(this).attr('id') == 'marquee_next_btn' ? 0 : 1;
			showad();
		});
		
		// 控制跑馬燈上下移動的處理函式
		function showad(){
			_lock = !_lock;
			var _now = $marqueeUl.position().top / _height;
			_now = (direction ? _now - 1 + $marqueeli.length : _now + 1)  % $marqueeli.length;
			
			// $marqueeUl 移動
			$marqueeUl.animate({
				top: _now * _height
			}, scrollSpeed, function(){
				// 如果已經移動到第二組時...則馬上把 top 設回到第一組的最後一筆
				// 藉此產生不間斷的輪播
				if(_now == $marqueeli.length - 1){
					$marqueeUl.css('top', $marqueeli.length / 2 * _height - _height);
				}else if(_now == 0){
					$marqueeUl.css('top', $marqueeli.length / 2 * _height);
				}
				_lock = !_lock;
			});
			
			// 再啟動計時器
			timer = setTimeout(showad, speed);
		}
		
		// 啟動計時器
		timer = setTimeout(showad, speed);

		$('a').focus(function(){
			this.blur();
		});
	});
</script>

function _openPageEditContent( _wpitem_uid )
{
	var open_uri = 'https://www.rt-mart.com.tw/direct/admweb/admweb.main.php?action=website_page_item_content' + 
					'&wpitem_uid=' + _wpitem_uid;
		
	var oOpenWindow = window.open(open_uri,
								'page_item_content',
								'toolbar=no,location=no,directories=no,status=no,menubar=no,scrollbars=yes,resizable=yes,copyhistory=no,width=780,height=580');
	oOpenWindow.focus();
}

function _openPageEditWindow( open_uri )
{
	var oOpenWindow = window.open(open_uri,
								'page_edit',
								'toolbar=no,location=no,directories=no,status=no,menubar=no,scrollbars=yes,resizable=yes,copyhistory=no,width=780,height=580');
	oOpenWindow.focus();
}

function _openImage( imgUri, imgPath ,imagewidth ,imageheight)
{
	imagewidth = imagewidth || 400; 
	imageheight = imageheight || 400; 
	if( !imgPath ) imgPath = '';
	var open_uri = 'https://www.rt-mart.com.tw/direct/openwin/openwin.index.php?action=images' + 
					'&imgUri=' + imgUri +
					'&imgPath=' + imgPath;

	var oOpenWindow = window.open(open_uri,
								'openImage',
								'toolbar=no,location=no,directories=no,status=no,menubar=no,scrollbars=no,resizable=yes,copyhistory=no,width='+imagewidth+',height='+imageheight );
	oOpenWindow.focus();
}

{
	var open_uri = 'https://www.rt-mart.com.tw/direct/openwin/openwin.index.php?action=product_sort' + 
					'&jsAct=' + jsAct +
					'&form_name=' + formName +

								'toolbar=no,location=no,directories=no,status=no,menubar=no,scrollbars=yes,resizable=yes,copyhistory=no,width=600,height=300');
	oOpenWindow.focus();
}

}

var menuDivId;
var menuDivId_out;
var objMenuDiv;
var objMenuDiv_out;
var _isMenuDiv = 0;

function showWebMenu( id ){
	menuDivId = 'div_webMenu_' + id;
	_isMenuDiv = 1;

	objMenu = document.getElementById( 'webMenu_' + id );
	objMenuDiv = document.getElementById( menuDivId );

	if( objMenuDiv ){
		graphics = ij_getXY(objMenu);
		
		objMenuDiv.style.left = graphics[0] + menu_move_x + 'px';
		objMenuDiv.style.top = graphics[1] + menu_move_y + 'px';	
		
		ij_showDiv(menuDivId, true);
	}
	
	if( objMenuDiv_out && menuDivId_out != menuDivId ){
		ij_showDiv(menuDivId_out, false);
	}
	menuDivId_out = null;
}

function removeWebMenu( id, timeOut ){
	menuDivId_out = 'div_webMenu_' + id;
	objMenuDiv_out = document.getElementById( 'div_webMenu_' + id );
	
	_isMenuDiv = 0;
	setTimeout( '_removeWebMenu()' ,timeOut);
}

function _removeWebMenu(){
	if( !_isMenuDiv && objMenuDiv_out )
		ij_showDiv(menuDivId_out, false);
}
/**/

String.prototype.trim = function() { return this.replace(/(^\s*)|(\s*$)/g, ""); }
function formatNumber(str) {
    str = str.trim();
    if(str.length <= 3){
        return str;
    }else{
        return formatNumber(str.substr(0,str.length-3))+','+str.substr(str.length-3);
    }
}
function RemoveStringComma(number) {
    var pos = number.indexOf(",");
    while (pos != -1) {
        number = number.substring(0, pos) + number.substring(pos + 1, number.length);
        pos = number.indexOf(",");
    }
    return number;
}

	//ul.appendChild(newli);
	
	
	
	$("#floorFix .icon-service").click( function() {
		if( /Android|webOS|iPhone|iPad|iPod|BlackBerry|IEMobile|Opera Mini/i.test(navigator.userAgent) ) {
			location.href = 'https://contact-center.hithot.cc/rt-mart/index.php?eservice=scrollbar&qaCategory=general';
		} else {
			show_off_srm();
		}
    });
	
	function show_off_srm(){
		var headerHeight = $('.rtmartHEADER').height() + 10;
		var windowHeight = window.innerHeight - 10;
		var windowWidth = $(window).width();
		var percentageWidth = 0.45;
		var buttonWidth = 45;
	
		if(document.getElementById('is_srm_load').value == '0'){
			document.getElementById('show_srm_outer').style.width = windowWidth*percentageWidth+buttonWidth+'px';
			document.getElementById('show_srm_outer').style.margin = headerHeight+'px 1px 1px 1px';
			document.getElementById('show_srm').style.width = windowWidth*percentageWidth+'px';
			document.getElementById('srm_iframe').style.width = windowWidth*percentageWidth+'px';
			document.getElementById('show_srm_outer').style.left = windowWidth - (windowWidth*percentageWidth+buttonWidth)+'px';
			document.getElementById('show_srm_outer').style.height = (windowHeight - headerHeight)+'px';
			document.getElementById('show_srm_outer').style.display = 'block';
			document.getElementById('is_srm_open').value = '1';
			
			document.getElementById('show_srm_outer').style.left = windowWidth - (windowWidth*percentageWidth+buttonWidth)+'px';
			document.getElementById('srm_iframe').style.display = 'block';
			document.getElementById('show_srm_outer').style.display = 'block';
			document.getElementById('is_srm_load').value = '1';
			document.getElementById('is_srm_open').value = '1';
			document.getElementById('srm_iframe').src = 'https://contact-center.hithot.cc/rt-mart/index.php?eservice=qrcode&qaCategory=general';

		} else {
			if(document.getElementById('is_srm_open').value == '1'){
				document.getElementById('show_srm_outer').style.left = windowWidth - buttonWidth+'px';
				document.getElementById('show_srm_outer').style.display = 'none';
				document.getElementById('is_srm_open').value = '0';
			} else {
				document.getElementById('show_srm_outer').style.width = windowWidth*percentageWidth+buttonWidth+'px';
				document.getElementById('show_srm_outer').style.margin = headerHeight+'px 1px 1px 1px';
				document.getElementById('show_srm').style.width = windowWidth*percentageWidth+'px';
				document.getElementById('srm_iframe').style.width = windowWidth*percentageWidth+'px';
				document.getElementById('show_srm_outer').style.left = windowWidth - (windowWidth*percentageWidth+buttonWidth)+'px';
				document.getElementById('show_srm_outer').style.height = (windowHeight - headerHeight)+'px';
				document.getElementById('show_srm_outer').style.display = 'block';
				document.getElementById('is_srm_open').value = '1';
			}	
		}
	}
</script>
